# Native implementation coverage

This is the implementation ledger required by `IMPLEMENTATION-HANDOFF.md`.
It measures the C17/OpenDoors candidate against the completed byte-pinned
3.6G analysis.  It does not reinterpret the analysis coverage ledgers as
native implementation evidence and does not combine unlike inventories into
an overall percentage.

## Status rules

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `missing` | No exact native C owner and exact native test are mapped yet. Existing nearby code does not change this status. |
| `candidate` | A named C owner exists, but comparison and/or exact native testing is incomplete. |
| `verified` | The declared bounded component has an exact native test or fixture and matches the final analysis for that scope. |
| `explicitly deferred` | The handoff or user explicitly deferred this scope; it is not silently counted as done. |

Any row promoted to `verified` must name the implementing C function and the
specific native test or fixture. A whole-program smoke test cannot verify an
unrelated child root, qualified transfer, failure prefix, or presentation
family.

Session proof is compositional. A monolithic interactive-session transcript
is not required: bounded deterministic native fixtures may prove connected
components, while presentation, persistence, input/runtime, RNG placement,
and physical adapters are proved independently and joined through their
declared seams.

`implementation-coverage/manifest.json` pins the SHA-256 of every imported
analysis artifact and refuses regeneration if the completed denominators
change. Regenerate with:

```sh
python3 tools/update_implementation_coverage.py
```

All 389 qualified `RMT-INIT.EXE` transfers now have candidate native owners.
This includes the complete entry/serial/status controller, three distinct
date-observation boundaries, deterministic world/file loops, every reset and
progress call, the helper bodies and returns, and completion/delay/terminal
tail. Candidate status does not waive the still-listed raw-state, alternate,
physical-failure, or Win32 prerequisites.

## Shared prerequisites

Ignored cleanup-CLOSE results remain part of the typed terminal carrier even
when they cannot change BASIC control flow: random CLOSE retains its retry DOS
byte, and sequential INPUT CLOSE, buffered PRINT, and sequential OUTPUT CLOSE
retain their cleanup DOS byte separately from the first failing operation.

| Runtime prerequisite | Native owner(s) | Current evidence | Status | Principal prerequisites |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| BRUN regular-file `PRINT`/`CLOSE` and shared CLOSE-all registry | `src/yt_file.c::{yt_database_random_close,yt_database_close_all_single,yt_database_close_all_method,yt_close_all_run,yt_database_set_close_provider,yt_radio_file_close}`, `src/yt_text.c::{yt_text_write,yt_text_append_line,yt_text_output_open,yt_text_output_open_append,yt_text_output_write,yt_text_device_print,yt_text_output_stage,yt_text_output_close,yt_text_output_close_all_method,yt_text_output_set_open_provider,yt_text_output_set_write_provider,yt_text_output_set_close_provider}`, `src/main_portname.c::{portname_close_all,main}`, `src/main_rmt_init.c::{read_handoff,credited_remote_name,rmt_close_all,main}`, `src/yt_init.c::{clear_yt_radio_messages,write_sequential_file,write_yt_auxiliary,write_rmt_auxiliary,yt_initialize_begin_yt,yt_initialize_world}`, `src/yt_names.c::{yt_names_write,yt_names_append,yt_names_create_default}`, `src/yt_session.c::{startup_configuration_close,session_close_file5,session_close_game_all,command_genesis,registration,lockout,append_news,radio_append_bytes,radio_read}`, `src/yt_maint.c::{yt_news_append,yt_maintenance_remove_alias,maintenance_radio_append_bytes,yt_radio_compact,invalidate_radio}`, `src/yt_main_error.c::yt_main_error_commit_fatal_to`, `src/yt_score.c::yt_score_generate` | The typed random-file transaction owns missing-file return, ordinary/device dispatch, every first DOS error byte, mandatory ignored-result retry, cleanup-before-ERR 70/57, and unregistered-handle residue. The typed sequential transaction accepts bytes into the live 128-byte buffer and flushes a full block only before accepting byte 129. Clear-carry short flushes retain exactly AX prefix bytes, discard the block, do not accept the triggering byte, drop registration, leave the handle orphaned, and route ERR 61 without a CLOSE retry. Carry accepts a DOS error byte plus either an observed accepted-prefix count and terminal cursor or the explicit unknown-prefix sentinel; both forms attempt one ignored-result cleanup close, drop registration, route ERR 71, and retain the observation after BASIC replaces the DOS result. The separate typed character-device reducer now performs one ordered one-byte write per logical byte, including the final completion write and an empty statement's zero-length write. It preserves the 24-bit index, byte column, stale one-byte buffer and pending-count distinction; accepts compatibility-bit zero-count drops as ordered subsequences; emits CR-only for SCRN/COM1/COM2 and CRLF for CONS/printers; maps value carry through DOS-major/extended AX to ERR 52/70; maps completion carry/short to ERR 57; and has no carrier-loss edge. Its fixture pins full success, both newline classes, a dropped byte, noncompatibility zero, index wrap, known and unknown carry effects, DOS-version mapping, completion residue, empty completion and provider rejection. On CLOSE the regular-file owner performs pending-buffer write, literal DOS EOF write, zero-length truncate, and first handle close; close-time shorts route ERR 61 without retry, while ordinary-disk carry at any operation retains the first DOS error/terminal cursor, attempts one ignored cleanup close, and routes ERR 70. Status-bit-80 character controls skip pending/EOF/truncate operations and map handle-close carry to ERR 57. Fixtures exhaust all 128 short-flush counts, all 255 DOS bytes for regular-file PRINT and every CLOSE ordinal, a known seven-byte/cursor-seven carry witness, both close-time short sites, success/truncation, cleanup carry/liveness, provider faults, missing explicit CLOSE, CLOSE-all identity, and native 300-byte durability; the DOS-EOF whole-file entry delegates to this owner while marker-free mode retains the raw writer. The typed APPEND OPEN tape owns access-2/access-1 fallback, initial/create/reopen classification, temporary-create close and ignored-result retry, error-5 extended classification, ignored query carry, device-configuration ERR 57, all three seek cuts, retained read prefixes and final-window refills through the terminal zero read. Fixtures exhaust every DOS byte for initial/create/reopen/temporary-close/read and each seek ordinal, both ERR 70/75 extended results, malformed providers, native missing-parent ERR 76, missing-file creation and exact cursor selection. APPEND then sends raw row plus CRLF through the same buffer and lets typed CLOSE append EOF and truncate the stale tail. This connects gameplay/maintenance news, YTNAME alias append, fatal ERRORS.DOR logging. deployed-fighter owner, encounter, DOUBLE damage, result/news,
fresh-persistence, Headquarters, and footer dispatch transaction.

binary-attacker news rows, the SINGLE/DOUBLE damage loop, bold result, fresh

post-player scan entries, every cached-player comparison, terminal-plus-one

news-before-direct pairs, and shields/fighters-only raw persistence. Its

## Promotion workflow

Work proceeds from shared prerequisites outward. For a bounded component:

1. identify its final analysis owner and all outgoing transfers;
2. name the exact C function(s), shared adapters, external effects and state;
3. add deterministic native tests for normal, alternate, interrupt, timeout,
   carrier, SysOp and physical-failure families that are reachable there;
4. compare remote bytes, local result, raw files/records, random draws and
   continuation state; and
5. update the root, transfer and presentation rows together. A component is
   not promoted while any reached child is replaced by an aggregate result.
